[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 3.4 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '---\r\nname: writing-plans\r\ndescription: Use when you have a spec or requirements for a multi-step task, before touching code\r\n---\r\n\r\n# Writing Plans\r\n\r\n## Overview\r\n\r\nWrite comprehensive implementation plans assuming the engineer has zero context for our codebase and questionable taste. Document everything they need to know: which files to touch for each task, code, testing, docs they might need to check, how to test it. Give them the whole plan as bite-sized tasks. DRY. YAGNI. TDD. Frequent commits.\r\n\r\nAssume they are a skilled developer, but know almost nothing about our toolset or problem domain. Assume they don\'t know good test design very well.\r\n\r\n**Announce at start:** "I\'m using the writing-plans skill to create the implementation plan."\r\n\r\n**Context:** If working in an isolated worktree, it should have been created via the `superpowers:using-git-worktrees` skill at execution time.\r\n\r\n**Save plans to:** `docs/superpowers/plans/YYYY-MM-DD-<feature-name>.md`\r\n- (User preferences for plan location override this default)\r\n\r\n## Scope Check\r\n\r\nIf the spec covers multiple independent subsystems, it should have been broken into sub-project specs during brainstorming. If it wasn\'t, suggest breaking this into separate plans — one per subsystem. Each plan should produce working, testable software on its own.\r\n\r\n## File Structure\r\n\r\nBefore defining tasks, map out which files will be created or modified and what each one is responsible for. This is where decomposition decisions get locked in.\r\n\r\n- Design units with clear boundaries and well-defined interfaces. Each file should have one clear responsibility.\r\n- You reason best about code you can hold in context at once, and your edits are more reliable when files are focused. Prefer smaller, focused files over large ones that do too much.\r\n- Files that change together should live together. Split by responsibility, not by technical layer.\r\n- In existing codebases, follow established patterns. If the codebase uses large files, don\'t unilaterally restructure - but if a file you\'re modifying has grown unwieldy, including a split in the plan is reasonable.\r\n\r\nThis structure informs the task decomposition. Each task should produce self-contained changes that make sense independently.\r\n\r\n## Task Right-Sizing\r\n\r\nA task is the smallest unit that carries its own test cycle and is worth a\r\nfresh reviewer\'s gate. When drawing task boundaries: fold setup,\r\nconfiguration, scaffolding, and documentation steps into the task whose\r\ndeliverable needs them; split only where a reviewer could meaningfully\r\nreject one task while approving its neighbor. Each task ends with an\r\nindependently testable deliverable.\r\n\r\n## Bite-Sized Task Granularity\r\n\r\n**Each step is one action (2-5 minutes):**\r\n- "Write the failing test" - step\r\n- "Run it to make sure it fails" - step\r\n- "Implement the minimal code to make the test pass" - step\r\n- "Run the tests and make sure they pass" - step\r\n- "Commit" - step\r\n\r\n## Plan Document Header\r\n\r\n**Every plan MUST start with this header:**\r\n\r\n```markdown\r\n# [Feature Name] Implementation Plan\r\n\r\n> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.\r\n\r\n**Goal:** [One sentence describing what this builds]\r\n\r\n**Architecture:** [2-3 sentences about approach]\r\n\r\n**Tech Stack:** [Key technologies/libraries]\r\n\r\n## Global Constraints\r\n\r\n[The spec\'s project-wide requirements — version floors, dependency limits,\r\nnaming and copy rules, platform requirements — one line each, with exact\r\nvalues copied verbatim from the spec. Every task\'s requirements implicitly\r\ninclude this section.]\r\n\r\n---\r\n```\r\n\r\n## Task Structure\r\n\r\n````markdown\r\n### Task N: [Component Name]\r\n\r\n**Files:**\r\n- Create: `exact/path/to/file.py`\r\n- Modify: `exact/path/to/existing.py:123-145`\r\n- Test: `tests/exact/path/to/test.py`\r\n\r\n**Interfaces:**\r\n- Consumes: [what this task uses from earlier tasks — exact signatures]\r\n- Produces: [what later tasks rely on — exact function names, parameter\r\n  and return types. A task\'s implementer sees only their own task; this\r\n  block is how they learn the names and types neighboring tasks use.]\r\n\r\n- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**\r\n\r\n```python\r\ndef test_specific_behavior():\r\n    result = function(input)\r\n    assert result == expected\r\n```\r\n\r\n- [ ] **Step 2: Run test to verify it fails**\r\n\r\nRun: `pytest tests/path/test.py::test_name -v`\r\nExpected: FAIL with "function not defined"\r\n\r\n- [ ] **Step 3: Write minimal implementation**\r\n\r\n```python\r\ndef function(input):\r\n    return expected\r\n```\r\n\r\n- [ ] **Step 4: Run test to verify it passes**\r\n\r\nRun: `pytest tests/path/test.py::test_name -v`\r\nExpected: PASS\r\n\r\n- [ ] **Step 5: Commit**\r\n\r\n```bash\r\ngit add tests/path/test.py src/path/file.py\r\ngit commit -m "feat: add specific feature"\r\n```\r\n````\r\n\r\n## No Placeholders\r\n\r\nEvery step must contain the actual content an engineer needs. These are **plan failures** — never write them:\r\n- "TBD", "TODO", "implement later", "fill in details"\r\n- "Add appropriate error handling" / "add validation" / "handle edge cases"\r\n- "Write tests for the above" (without actual test code)\r\n- "Similar to Task N" (repeat the code — the engineer may be reading tasks out of order)\r\n- Steps that describe what to do without showing how (code blocks required for code steps)\r\n- References to types, functions, or methods not defined in any task\r\n\r\n## Remember\r\n- Exact file paths always\r\n- Complete code in every step — if a step changes code, show the code\r\n- Exact commands with expected output\r\n- DRY, YAGNI, TDD, frequent commits\r\n\r\n## Self-Review\r\n\r\nAfter writing the complete plan, look at the spec with fresh eyes and check the plan against it. This is a checklist you run yourself — not a subagent dispatch.\r\n\r\n**1. Spec coverage:** Skim each section/requirement in the spec. Can you point to a task that implements it? List any gaps.\r\n\r\n**2. Placeholder scan:** Search your plan for red flags — any of the patterns from the "No Placeholders" section above. Fix them.\r\n\r\n**3. Type consistency:** Do the types, method signatures, and property names you used in later tasks match what you defined in earlier tasks? A function called `clearLayers()` in Task 3 but `clearFullLayers()` in Task 7 is a bug.\r\n\r\nIf you find issues, fix them inline. No need to re-review — just fix and move on. If you find a spec requirement with no task, add the task.\r\n\r\n## Execution Handoff\r\n\r\nAfter saving the plan, offer execution choice:\r\n\r\n**"Plan complete and saved to `docs/superpowers/plans/<filename>.md`. Two execution options:**\r\n\r\n**1. Subagent-Driven (recommended)** - I dispatch a fresh subagent per task, review between tasks, fast iteration\r\n\r\n**2. Inline Execution** - Execute tasks in this session using executing-plans, batch execution with checkpoints\r\n\r\n**Which approach?"**\r\n\r\n**If Subagent-Driven chosen:**\r\n- **REQUIRED SUB-SKILL:** Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development\r\n- Fresh subagent per task + two-stage review\r\n\r\n**If Inline Execution chosen:**\r\n- **REQUIRED SUB-SKILL:** Use superpowers:executing-plans\r\n- Batch execution with checkpoints for review\r\n\r\n'}]

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 3.3 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '// Admin-only booking pass management: create / list / revoke / rotate.\r\n// Every issued link uses the strong /book/:slug public route.\r\nimport { createClient } from "npm:@supabase/supabase-js@2";\r\nimport { corsHeaders } from "npm:@supabase/supabase-js@2/cors";\r\nimport { generateBookingSlug, validateNewPassInput } from "../_shared/bookingPassLinks.ts";\r\nimport { deleteEvent } from "../_shared/googleCalendar.ts";\r\nimport { findAuthUserIdByEmail } from "../_shared/linkPassToAccount.ts";\r\n\r\nasync function sha256Hex(input: string): Promise<string> {\r\n  const buf = await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", new TextEncoder().encode(input));\r\n  return Array.from(new Uint8Array(buf)).map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join("");\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction genToken(): string {\r\n  const bytes = new Uint8Array(32);\r\n  crypto.getRandomValues(bytes);\r\n  return btoa(String.fromCharCode(...bytes))\r\n    .replace(/\\+/g, "-").replace(/\\//g, "_").replace(/=+$/g, "");\r\n}\r\n\r\nconst APP_ORIGIN = Deno.env.get("APP_ORIGIN") || "https://lsatprep.study";\r\n\r\nfunction bookingUrl(slug: string): string {\r\n  return `${APP_ORIGIN}/book/${slug}`;\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction json(body: unknown, status = 200) {\r\n  return new Response(JSON.stringify(body), {\r\n    status,\r\n    headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" },\r\n  });\r\n}\r\n\r\nDeno.serve(async (req) => {\r\n  if (req.method === "OPTIONS") return new Response("ok", { headers: corsHeaders });\r\n  try {\r\n    const authHeader = req.headers.get("Authorization") ?? "";\r\n    if (!authHeader.startsWith("Bearer ")) return json({ error: "unauthorized" }, 401);\r\n    const token = authHeader.slice(7);\r\n\r\n    const supabase = createClient(\r\n      Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_URL")!,\r\n      Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY")!,\r\n    );\r\n    // Validate the caller JWT with the anon client: getClaims() supports the\r\n    // asymmetric signing-keys system, unlike getUser() on a service-role client.\r\n    const authClient = createClient(\r\n      Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_URL")!,\r\n      Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_ANON_KEY")!,\r\n    );\r\n    const { data: claimsRes } = await authClient.auth.getClaims(token);\r\n    const user = claimsRes?.claims?.sub ? { id: String(claimsRes.claims.sub) } : null;\r\n    if (!user) return json({ error: "unauthorized" }, 401);\r\n    const { data: roleRow, error: roleError } = await supabase\r\n      .from("user_roles").select("role").eq("user_id", user.id).eq("role", "admin").maybeSingle();\r\n    if (roleError) throw roleError;\r\n    if (!roleRow) return json({ error: "forbidden" }, 403);\r\n\r\n    const body = await req.json().catch(() => ({}));\r\n    const action = String(body.action || "");\r\n\r\n    const singleSessionDto = (row: any, association?: "verified_account" | "possible_match_unverified_email") => ({\r\n      source: "single_session" as const,\r\n      id: row.id,\r\n      start_at: row.start_at,\r\n      end_at: row.end_at,\r\n      status: row.status,\r\n      meet_url: row.status === "booked" ? row.meet_url ?? null : null,\r\n      name: row.name ?? null,\r\n      email: row.email ?? null,\r\n      user_id: row.user_id ?? null,\r\n      created_at: row.created_at,\r\n      ...(association ? { association } : {}),\r\n    });\r\n\r\n    if (action === "single_sessions_global") {\r\n      const { data, error } = await supabase.from("single_session_bookings")\r\n        .select("id,start_at,end_at,status,meet_url,name,email,user_id,created_at")\r\n        .order("start_at", { ascending: false })\r\n        .limit(500);\r\n      if (error) throw error;\r\n      return json({ bookings: (data ?? []).map((row: any) => singleSessionDto(row)) });\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    if (action === "list") {\r\n      const { data, error } = await supabase\r\n        .from("booking_passes")\r\n        .select("*")\r\n        .order("created_at", { ascending: false });\r\n      if (error) throw error;\r\n      return json({ passes: data ?? [] });\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    if (action === "history") {\r\n      const passId = String(body.pass_id || "");\r\n      if (!passId) return json({ error: "pass_id_required" }, 400);\r\n      const { data, error } = await supabase\r\n        .from("booking_pass_redemptions")\r\n        .select("*")\r\n        .eq("booking_pass_id", passId)\r\n        .order("start_at", { ascending: false });\r\n      if (error) throw error;\r\n      return json({ redemptions: data ?? [] });\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    if (action === "list_students") {\r\n      // Every real student account, for the New Pass picker. Admins excluded.\r\n      const { data: profiles, error: pErr } = await supabase\r\n        .from("profiles").select("class_id, display_name");\r\n      if (pErr) throw pErr;\r\n      const { data: roles } = await supabase.from("user_roles").select("user_id, role").eq("role", "admin");\r\n      const adminIds = new Set((roles ?? []).map((r: { user_id: string }) => r.user_id));\r\n      const { data: authRes, error: aErr } = await supabase.auth.admin.listUsers({ perPage: 1000 });\r\n      if (aErr) throw aErr;\r\n      const emailMap = new Map(authRes.users.map((u: { id: string; email?: string }) => [u.id, u.email ?? null]));\r\n      const students = (profiles ?? [])\r\n        .filter((p: { class_id: string }) => !adminIds.has(p.class_id))\r\n        .map((p: { class_id: string; display_name: string | null }) => ({\r\n          id: p.class_id,\r\n          name: p.display_name || null,\r\n          email: emailMap.get(p.class_id) ?? null,\r\n        }))\r\n        .sort((a, b) => (a.name || a.email || "").localeCompare(b.name || b.email || ""));\r\n      return json({ students });\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    if (action === "create") {\r\n      const validated = validateNewPassInput({\r\n        totalCredits: body.total_credits,\r\n        sessionLengthMinutes: body.session_length_minutes,\r\n        expiresAt: body.expires_at,\r\n      });\r\n      if (!validated.ok) return json({ error: validated.error }, 400);\r\n      const { totalCredits, sessionLengthMinutes: sessionLen, expiresAt } = validated.value;\r\n      const raw = genToken();\r\n      const hash = await sha256Hex(raw);\r\n\r\n      // Passes created by name/email only are invisible to the signed-in\r\n      // surfaces (in-app booking page, uploads, schedule history). If the email\r\n      // matches a real account, attach it now so both doors agree.\r\n      if (!body.student_id && body.student_email) {\r\n        body.student_id = await findAuthUserIdByEmail(supabase, body.student_email);\r\n      }\r\n\r\n      // When granting to a real student account, go through grant_booking_access\r\n      // so any prior active pass is atomically revoked (one-active-per-student)\r\n      // AND the public booking slug is created in the same transaction.\r\n      if (body.student_id) {\r\n        // Generate slug + retry on the (rare) unique collision.\r\n        let lastErr: unknown = null;\r\n        for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 3; attempt++) {\r\n          const slug = generateBookingSlug(body.student_name, body.student_email);\r\n          const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc("grant_booking_access", {\r\n            _student_id: body.student_id,\r\n            _student_name: body.student_name || null,\r\n            _student_email: body.student_email || null,\r\n            _total_credits: totalCredits,\r\n            _session_length_minutes: sessionLen,\r\n            _expires_at: expiresAt,\r\n            _created_by: user.id,\r\n            _token_hash: hash,\r\n            _booking_slug: slug,\r\n          });\r\n          if (!error) return json({ pass: data, booking_url: bookingUrl(slug) });\r\n          const msg = String(error.message || "");\r\n          if (msg.includes("student_id_required")) return json({ error: "student_id_required" }, 400);\r\n          if ((error as { code?: string }).code === "23505") { lastErr = error; continue; } // slug collision → retry\r\n          throw error;\r\n        }\r\n        console.error("booking slug collision", lastErr);\r\n        return json({ error: "slug_collision" }, 409);\r\n      }\r\n\r\n      // Manual passes use the same strong public-link path as account-backed passes.\r\n      let lastErr: unknown = null;\r\n      for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 3; attempt++) {\r\n        const slug = generateBookingSlug(body.student_name, body.student_email);\r\n        const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc("create_manual_booking_pass", {\r\n          _student_name: body.student_name || null,\r\n          _student_email: body.student_email || null,\r\n          _total_credits: totalCredits,\r\n          _session_length_minutes: sessionLen,\r\n          _expires_at: expiresAt,\r\n          _notes: body.notes || null,\r\n          _created_by: user.id,\r\n          _token_hash: hash,\r\n          _booking_slug: slug,\r\n        });\r\n        if (!error) return json({ pass: data, booking_url: bookingUrl(slug) });\r\n        if ((error as { code?: string }).code === "23505") { lastErr = error; continue; }\r\n        throw error;\r\n      }\r\n      console.error("booking slug collision", lastErr);\r\n      return json({ error: "slug_collision" }, 409);\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    if (action === "get_availability") {\r\n      const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc("get_booking_availability");\r\n      if (error) throw error;\r\n      return json(data ?? { timezone: "America/Toronto", ranges: [] });\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    if (action === "set_availability") {\r\n      const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc("set_booking_availability", {\r\n        _timezone: body.timezone || null,\r\n        _ranges: body.ranges ?? [],\r\n      });\r\n      if (error) throw error;\r\n      return json(data ?? { timezone: "America/Toronto", ranges: [] });\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    if (action === "get_for_student") {\r\n      const studentId = String(body.student_id || "");\r\n      if (!studentId) return json({ error: "student_id_required" }, 400);\r\n      const { data, error } = await supabase\r\n        .from("booking_passes")\r\n        .select("id,total_credits,used_credits,session_length_minutes,status,expires_at,created_at,booking_slug")\r\n        .eq("student_id", studentId)\r\n        .in("status", ["active", "paused", "exhausted", "expired"])\r\n        .order("created_at", { ascending: false })\r\n        .limit(1)\r\n        .maybeSingle();\r\n      if (error) throw error;\r\n      const { data: verifiedRows, error: verifiedError } = await supabase.from("single_session_bookings")\r\n        .select("id,start_at,end_at,status,meet_url,name,email,user_id,created_at")\r\n        .eq("user_id", studentId)\r\n        .order("start_at", { ascending: false });\r\n      if (verifiedError) throw verifiedError;\r\n      const { data: authUser, error: authUserError } = await supabase.auth.admin.getUserById(studentId);\r\n      if (authUserError) throw authUserError;\r\n      let possibleRows: any[] = [];\r\n      if (authUser?.user?.email) {\r\n        const { data: emailRows, error: emailError } = await supabase.from("single_session_bookings")\r\n          .select("id,start_at,end_at,status,meet_url,name,email,user_id,created_at")\r\n          .ilike("email", authUser.user.email.trim())\r\n          .order("start_at", { ascending: false });\r\n        if (emailError) throw emailError;\r\n        possibleRows = (emailRows ?? []).filter((row: any) => row.user_id !== studentId);\r\n      }\r\n      const associations = {\r\n        single_session_bookings: (verifiedRows ?? []).map((row: any) => singleSessionDto(row, "verified_account")),\r\n        single_session_possible_matches: possibleRows.map((row: any) => singleSessionDto(row, "possible_match_unverified_email")),\r\n      };\r\n      if (!data) return json({ pass: null, ...associations });\r\n      return json({\r\n        pass: {\r\n          id: data.id,\r\n          total_credits: data.total_credits,\r\n          used_credits: data.used_credits,\r\n          credits_remaining: Math.max(0, data.total_credits - data.used_credits),\r\n          session_length_minutes: data.session_length_minutes,\r\n          status: data.status,\r\n          expires_at: data.expires_at,\r\n          booking_url: data.booking_slug ? bookingUrl(data.booking_slug) : null,\r\n        },\r\n        ...associations,\r\n      });\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    if (action === "regenerate_link") {\r\n      const studentId = String(body.student_id || "");\r\n      if (!studentId) return json({ error: "student_id_required" }, 400);\r\n      // Rotate slug + token_hash atomically; retry on the rare slug collision.\r\n      let lastErr: unknown = null;\r\n      // Need the current name/email for a nice slug prefix.\r\n      const { data: cur } = await supabase\r\n        .from("booking_passes").select("student_name,student_email")\r\n        .eq("student_id", studentId).in("status", ["active", "paused"])\r\n        .order("created_at", { ascending: false }).limit(1).maybeSingle();\r\n      for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 3; attempt++) {\r\n        const slug = generateBookingSlug(cur?.student_name, cur?.student_email);\r\n        const newHash = await sha256Hex(genToken());\r\n        const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc("regenerate_booking_link", {\r\n          _student_id: studentId, _new_slug: slug, _new_token_hash: newHash,\r\n        });\r\n        if (!error) return json({ booking_url: bookingUrl(String(data)) });\r\n        const msg = String(error.message || "");\r\n        if (msg.includes("no_active_pass") || msg.includes("no_resumable_pass")) {\r\n          return json({ error: "no_resumable_pass" }, 404);\r\n        }\r\n        if ((error as { code?: string }).code === "23505") { lastErr = error; continue; }\r\n        throw error;\r\n      }\r\n      console.error("booking slug collision", lastErr);\r\n      return json({ error: "slug_collision" }, 409);\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    if (action === "update") {\r\n      const passId = String(body.pass_id || "");\r\n      if (!passId) return json({ error: "pass_id_required" }, 400);\r\n      const { data: cur, error: curErr } = await supabase\r\n        .from("booking_passes").select("*").eq("id", passId).single();\r\n      if (curErr) throw curErr;\r\n\r\n      let requestedRemaining: number | null = null;\r\n      if (body.total_credits !== undefined) {\r\n        const total = Number(body.total_credits);\r\n        if (!Number.isInteger(total) || total < 1 || total > 50) return json({ error: "invalid_credits" }, 400);\r\n        requestedRemaining = Math.max(0, total - cur.used_credits);\r\n      }\r\n      let requestedSessionLength: number | null = null;\r\n      if (body.session_length_minutes !== undefined) {\r\n        const sessionLen =   }\r\n        requestedSessionLength = sessionLen;\r\n      }\r\n      let requestedExpiry: string | null = null;\r\n      let clearExpiry = false;\r\n      if (body.expires_at !== undefined) {\r\n        if (body.expires_at === null || body.expires_at === "") {\r\n          clearExpiry = true;\r\n        } else if (typeof body.expires_at !== "string" || "pause" : "resume",\r\n      });\r\n      if (error) throw error;\r\n      return json({ pass: data });\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    if (action === "list_bookings") {\r\n      const { data, error } = await supabase\r\n        .from("booking_pass_redemptions")\r\n        .select("*, booking_passes(id, student_name, student_email, student_id, session_length_minutes), external_work_uploads(id, file_name)")\r\n        .order("start_at", { ascending: false })\r\n        .limit(500);\r\n      if (error) throw error;\r\n      // Flatten to an upload_count so the client doesn\'t ship file lists it\r\n      // won\'t render until the row is expanded (files are fetched on demand).\r\n      const bookings = (data ?? []).map((b: any) => ({\r\n        ...b,\r\n        upload_count: Array.isArray(b.external_work_uploads) ? b.external_work_uploads.length : 0,\r\n        external_work_uploads: undefined,\r\n      }));\r\n      return json({ bookings });\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    if (action === "get_booking_health") {\r\n      const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc("get_booking_health");\r\n      if (error) throw error;\r\n      return json(data);\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    // Files a student attached to a specific booking, with short-lived signed\r\n    // download URLs (private bucket). Admin-only; RLS is bypassed by the service\r\n    // role, so the admin gate above is the access control.\r\n    if (action === "get_booking_uploads") {\r\n      const redemptionId = String(body.redemption_id || "");\r\n      if (!redemptionId) return json({ error: "redemption_id_required" }, 400);\r\n      const { data: rows, error } = await supabase\r\n        .from("external_work_uploads")\r\n        .select("id, file_name, file_type, storage_path, created_at")\r\n        .eq("redemption_id", redemptionId)\r\n        .order("created_at", { ascending: true });\r\n      if (error) throw error;\r\n      const uploads = [];\r\n      for (const r of rows ?? []) {\r\n        const { data: signed } = await supabase.storage\r\n          .from("external-work-uploads")\r\n          .createSignedUrl(r.storage_path, 600); // 10 min\r\n        uploads.push({\r\n          id: r.id, file_name: r.file_name, file_type: r.file_type,\r\n          created_at: r.created_at, url: signed?.signedUrl ?? null,\r\n        });\r\n      }\r\n      return json({ uploads });\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    if (action === "cancel_booking") {\r\n      const redemptionId = String(body.redemption_id || "");\r\n      if (!redemptionId) return json({ error: "redemption_id_required" }, 400);\r\n      const { data: result, error } = await supabase.rpc("cancel_booking_redemption", {\r\n        _redemption_id: redemptionId, _refund: true, _actor_id: user.id,\r\n      });\r\n      if (error) throw error;\r\n      const redemption = result?.redemption;\r\n      if (redemption?.calendar_action === "delete" && redemption.google_event_id) {\r\n        const { data: state, error: stateErr } = await supabase.from("gcal_sync_state")\r\n          .select("calendar_id").eq("id", 1).maybeSingle();\r\n        if (stateErr) throw stateErr;\r\n        try {\r\n          await deleteEvent(state?.calendar_id || "primary", redemption.google_event_id, { sendUpdates: "all" });\r\n          const { error: persistErr } = await supabase.from("booking_pass_redemptions").update({\r\n            calendar_action: "none", calendar_status: "synced", calendar_error: null,\r\n            calendar_last_synced_at: new Date().toISOString(), needs_gcal_sync: false,\r\n            gcal_state: "synced", gcal_error: null,\r\n          }).eq("id", redemptionId);\r\n          if (persistErr) throw persistErr;\r\n          redemption.calendar_action = "none";\r\n          redemption.calendar_status = "synced";\r\n        } catch (calendarError) {\r\n          const message = calendarError instanceof Error ? calendarError.message : String(calendarError);\r\n          const { error: persistErr } = await supabase.from("booking_pass_redemptions").update({\r\n            calendar_action: "delete", calendar_status: "pending", calendar_error: message,\r\n            needs_gcal_sync: true, gcal_state: "pending", gcal_error: message,\r\n          }).eq("id", redemptionId);\r\n          if (persistErr) throw persistErr;\r\n          redemption.calendar_status = "pending";\r\n        }\r\n      }\r\n      return json({ ...result, redemption });\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    if (action === "mark_no_show") {\r\n      const redemptionId = String(body.redemption_id || "");\r\n      if (!redemptionId) return json({ error: "redemption_id_required" }, 400);\r\n      const { data, error } = await supabase\r\n        .from("booking_pass_redemptions")\r\n        .update({ status: "no_show" })\r\n        .eq("id", redemptionId)\r\n        .eq("status", "booked")\r\n        .select().single();\r\n      if (error) throw error;\r\n      return json({ redemption: data });\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    if (action === "rotate") {\r\n      const passId = String(body.pass_id || "");\r\n      if (!passId) return json({ error: "pass_id_required" }, 400);\r\n      const { data: cur, error: curErr } = await supabase\r\n        .from("booking_passes").select("*").eq("id", passId).single();\r\n      if (curErr) throw curErr;\r\n\r\n      // The slug is the Bearer [REDACTED], so every pass rotates through a slug RPC.\r\n      // This also upgrades legacy manual passes that never received a slug.\r\n      if (!cur.student_id) {\r\n        let lastErr: unknown = null;\r\n        for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 3; attempt++) {\r\n          const slug = generateBookingSlug(cur.student_name, cur.student_email);\r\n          const newHash = await sha256Hex(genToken());\r\n          const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc("regenerate_manual_booking_link", {\r\n            _pass_id: passId, _new_slug: slug, _new_token_hash: newHash,\r\n          });\r\n          if (!error) return json({ booking_url: bookingUrl(String(data)) });\r\n          const msg = String(error.message || "");\r\n          if (msg.includes("no_resumable_pass")) return json({ error: "no_resumable_pass" }, 404);\r\n          if ((error as { code?: string }).code === "23505") { lastErr = error; continue; }\r\n          throw error;\r\n        }\r\n        console.error("booking slug collision", lastErr);\r\n        return json({ error: "slug_collision" }, 409);\r\n      }\r\n      if (cur.student_id) {\r\n        let lastErr: unknown = null;\r\n        for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 3; attempt++) {\r\n          const slug = generateBookingSlug(cur.student_name, cur.student_email);\r\n          const newHash = await sha256Hex(genToken());\r\n          const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc("regenerate_booking_link", {\r\n            _student_id: cur.student_id, _new_slug: slug, _new_token_hash: newHash,\r\n          });\r\n          if (!error) return json({ booking_url: bookingUrl(String(data)) });\r\n          const msg = String(error.message || "");\r\n          if (msg.includes("no_resumable_pass")) return json({ error: "no_resumable_pass" }, 404);\r\n          if ((error as { code?: string }).code === "23505") { lastErr = error; continue; }\r\n          throw error;\r\n        }\r\n        console.error("booking slug collision", lastErr);\r\n        return json({ error: "slug_collision" }, 409);\r\n      }\r\n      return json({ error: "no_resumable_pass" }, 404);\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    if (action === "revoke") {\r\n      const passId = String(body.pass_id || "");\r\n      if (!passId) return json({ error: "pass_id_required" }, 400);\r\n      const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc("manage_booking_pass", {\r\n        _pass_id: passId, _action: "revoke",\r\n      });\r\n      if (error) throw error;\r\n      return json({ pass: data });\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    return json({ error: "unknown_action" }, 400);\r\n  } catch (e) {\r\n    console.error("booking-pass-admin error", e);\r\n    return json({ error: e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e) }, 500);\r\n  }\r\n});\r\n\r\n'}]

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 3.7 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";\r\nimport { supabase } from "@/integrations/supabase/client";\r\nimport { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";\r\nimport { Card } from "@/components/ui/card";\r\nimport { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";\r\nimport { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";\r\nimport { Switch } from "@/components/ui/switch";\r\nimport { toast } from "sonner";\r\nimport { Loader2, Save, Trash2 } from "lucide-react";\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * Who can book what, and at what price.\r\n *\r\n * This is the screen that replaces making a whole new Cal.com event type every\r\n * time one person pays a different rate. Three things live here:\r\n *\r\n *   - the events themselves (length, buffers, window, default price)\r\n *   - per-person prices, keyed by email because a booker need not be signed in\r\n *   - per-student offers: which offers a signed-in student sees at all\r\n *\r\n * Everything writes straight to the tables under admin RLS. There is no edge\r\n * function in the way, so what this screen shows is what the booking page reads.\r\n */\r\n\r\ntype EventType = {\r\n  slug: string;\r\n  label: string;\r\n  length_minutes: number;\r\n  slot_interval_minutes: number | null;\r\n  minimum_notice_minutes: number;\r\n  buffer_before_minutes: number;\r\n  buffer_after_minutes: number;\r\n  window_business_days: number;\r\n  price_cents: number;\r\n  currency: string;\r\n  requires_payment: boolean;\r\n  active: boolean;\r\n};\r\n\r\ntype PriceOverride = { email: string; event_slug: string; price_cents: number; note: string | null };\r\n\r\ntype Offer = { id: string; label: string; provider: string; url: string; credits: number; active: boolean };\r\n\r\ntype StudentOffer = { student_id: string; offer_id: string; enabled: boolean; url_override: string | null };\r\n\r\ntype Student = { class_id: string; display_name: string | null };\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * booking_event_types and booking_price_overrides landed after the last types\r\n * generation, so the typed client does not know their names yet. Casting here\r\n * beats hand-editing the generated types file, which would be overwritten the\r\n * next time it is regenerated. Drop this once the types catch up.\r\n */\r\n// eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any\r\ntype LooseTable = any;\r\nconst db = supabase as unknown as { from: (table: string) => LooseTable };\r\n\r\nconst dollars = (cents: number) => (cents / 100).toFixed(2);\r\nconst cents = (value: string) => Math.round(Number(value || "0") * 100);\r\n\r\nexport default function BookingAccess() {\r\n  const [events, setEvents] = useState<EventType[]>([]);\r\n  const [overrides, setOverrides] = useState<PriceOverride[]>([]);\r\n  const [offers, setOffers] = useState<Offer[]>([]);\r\n  const [students, setStudents] = useState<Student[]>([]);\r\n  const [studentOffers, setStudentOffers] = useState<StudentOffer[]>([]);\r\n  const [picked, setPicked] = useState<string>("");\r\n  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);\r\n  const [saving, setSaving] = useState<string | null>(null);\r\n\r\n  // New price override, held separately so a half-typed row never writes.\r\n  const [newEmail, setNewEmail] = useState("");\r\n  const [newEvent, setNewEvent] = useState("single-session");\r\n  const [newPrice, setNewPrice] = useState("");\r\n\r\n  const load = useCallback(async () => {\r\n    setLoading(true);\r\n    try {\r\n      const [e, o, off, st] = await Promise.all([\r\n        db.from("booking_event_types").select("*").order("slug"),\r\n        db.from("booking_price_overrides").select("*").order("email"),\r\n        db.from("booking_offers").select("id,label,provider,url,credits,active").order("id"),\r\n        supabase.from("profiles").select("class_id,display_name").order("display_name"),\r\n      ]);\r\n      if (e.error) throw e.error;\r\n      setEvents((e.data ?? []) as EventType[]);\r\n      setOverrides((o.data ?? []) as PriceOverride[]);\r\n      setOffers((off.data ?? []) as Offer[]);\r\n      setStudents((st.data ?? []) as Student[]);\r\n    } catch (error) {\r\n      toast.error(error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Could not load booking access");\r\n    } finally {\r\n      setLoading(false);\r\n    }\r\n  }, []);\r\n\r\n  useEffect(() => { void load(); }, [load]);\r\n\r\n  // A student with no rows at all is on the default arrangement: every active\r\n  // offer. Rows only appear once Joshua has decided something for them.\r\n  useEffect(() => {\r\n    if (!picked) { setStudentOffers([]); return; }\r\n    void (async () => {\r\n      const { data, error } = await db\r\n        .from("student_booking_offers")\r\n        .select("student_id,offer_id,enabled,url_override")\r\n        .eq("student_id", picked);\r\n      if (error) { toast.error(error.message); return; }\r\n      setStudentOffers((data ?? []) as StudentOffer[]);\r\n    })();\r\n  }, [picked]);\r\n\r\n  const saveEvent = async (event: EventType) => {\r\n    setSaving(event.slug);\r\n    const { error } = await db\r\n      .from("booking_event_types")\r\n      .update({\r\n        price_cents: event.price_cents,\r\n        length_minutes: event.length_minutes,\r\n        buffer_before_minutes: event.buffer_before_minutes,\r\n        buffer_after_minutes: event.buffer_after_minutes,\r\n        window_business_days: event.window_business_days,\r\n        minimum_notice_minutes: event.minimum_notice_minutes,\r\n        active: event.active,\r\n      } as never)\r\n      .eq("slug", event.slug);\r\n    setSaving(null);\r\n    if (error) { toast.error(error.message); return; }\r\n    toast.success(`${event.label} saved`);\r\n  };\r\n\r\n  const addOverride = async () => {\r\n    const email = newEmail.trim().toLowerCase();\r\n    if (!email || !newPrice.trim()) { toast.error("Email and price are both needed"); return; }\r\n    setSaving("override");\r\n    const { error } = await db\r\n      .from("booking_price_overrides")\r\n      .upsert({ email, event_slug: newEvent, price_cents: cents(newPrice) } as never,\r\n        { onConflict: "email,event_slug" });\r\n    setSaving(null);\r\n    if (error) { toast.error(error.message); return; }\r\n    setNewEmail(""); setNewPrice("");\r\n    toast.success(`${email} pays $${Number(newPrice).toFixed(2)}`);\r\n    await load();\r\n  };\r\n\r\n  const removeOverride = async (row: PriceOverride) => {\r\n    const { error } = await db\r\n      .from("booking_price_overrides").delete()\r\n      .eq("email", row.email).eq("event_slug", row.event_slug);\r\n    if (error) { toast.error(error.message); return; }\r\n    toast.success("Back to the default price");\r\n    await load();\r\n  };\r\n\r\n  const setOfferForStudent = async (offerId: string, enabled: boolean) => {\r\n    if (!picked) return;\r\n    const { error } = await db\r\n      .from("student_booking_offers")\r\n      .upsert({ student_id: picked, offer_id: offerId, enabled } as never,\r\n        { onConflict: "student_id,offer_id" });\r\n    if (error) { toast.error(error.message); return; }\r\n    setStudentOffers((rows) => {\r\n      const next = rows.filter((row) => row.offer_id !== offerId);\r\n      return [...next, { student_id: picked, offer_id: offerId, enabled, url_override: null }];\r\n    });\r\n  };\r\n\r\n  const pickedStudent = useMemo(\r\n    () => students.find((student) => student.class_id === picked) ?? null,\r\n    [picked, students],\r\n  );\r\n\r\n  if (loading) {\r\n    return <main className="min-h-screen p-8"><Loader2 className="h-6 w-6 animate-spin" /></main>;\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  return (\r\n    <main className="min-h-screen space-y-8 p-6 sm:p-10">\r\n      <header>\r\n        <h1 className="text-2xl font-semibold tracking-tight">Booking access</h1>\r\n        <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted-foreground">\r\n          What each event is, what it costs, and who is allowed to book it.\r\n        </p>\r\n      </header>\r\n\r\n      <section className="space-y-4">\r\n        <h2 className="text-lg font-medium">Events</h2>\r\n        {events.map((event) => (\r\n          <Card key={event.slug} className="p-4">\r\n            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-end gap-4">\r\n              <div className="min-w-40">\r\n                <div className="font-medium">{event.label}</div>\r\n                <div className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{event.slug}</div>\r\n              </div>\r\n              <Field label="Price ($)" value={dollars(event.price_cents)} disabled={!event.requires_payment}\r\n                onChange={(v) => setEvents((rows) => rows.map((r) => r.slug === event.slug ? { ...r, price_cents: cents(v) } : r))} />\r\n              <Field label="Minutes" value={String(event.length_minutes)}\r\n                onChange={(v) => setEvents((rows) => rows.map((r) => r.slug === event.slug ? { ...r, length_minutes: Number(v || 0) } : r))} />\r\n              <Field label="Buffer before" value={String(event.buffer_before_minutes)}\r\n                onChange={(v) => setEvents((rows) => rows.map((r) => r.slug === event.slug ? { ...r, buffer_before_minutes: Number(v || 0) } : r))} />\r\n              <Field label="Buffer after" value={String(event.buffer_after_minutes)}\r\n                onChange={(v) => setEvents((rows) => rows.map((r) => r.slug === event.slug ? { ...r, buffer_after_minutes: Number(v || 0) } : r))} />\r\n              <Field label="Days ahead" value={String(event.window_business_days)}\r\n                onChange={(v) => setEvents((rows) => rows.map((r) => r.slug === event.slug ? { ...r, window_business_days: Number(v || 0) } : r))} />\r\n              <Button onClick={() => void saveEvent(event)} disabled={saving === event.slug}>\r\n                {saving === event.slug ? <Loader2 className="mr-2 h-4 w-4 animate-spin" /> : <Save className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" />}\r\n                Save\r\n              </Button>\r\n            </div>\r\n            {!event.requires_payment ? (\r\n              <p className="mt-3 text-xs text-muted-foreground">Free event. Price is ignored and no payment is taken.</p>\r\n            ) : null}\r\n          </Card>\r\n        ))}\r\n      </section>\r\n\r\n      <section className="space-y-4">\r\n        <h2 className="text-lg font-medium">Prices for particular people</h2>\r\n        <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">\r\n          Overrides the event price for one email. This is what a reduced rate is: one row, not a new event.\r\n        </p>\r\n        <Card className="flex flex-wrap items-end gap-3 p-4">\r\n          <div className="grow">\r\n            <Label htmlFor="override-email">Email</Label>\r\n            <Input id="override-email" value={newEmail} onChange={(e) => setNewEmail(e.target.value)} placeholder="student@example.com" />\r\n          </div>\r\n          <div>\r\n            <Label htmlFor="override-event">Event</Label>\r\n            <select id="override-event" className="h-10 rounded-md border border-input bg-background px-3 text-sm"\r\n              value={newEvent} onChange={(e) => setNewEvent(e.target.value)}>\r\n              {events.filter((event) => event.requires_payment).map((event) => (\r\n                <option key={event.slug} value={event.slug}>{event.label}</option>\r\n              ))}\r\n            </select>\r\n          </div>\r\n          <div className="w-28">\r\n            <Label htmlFor="override-price">Price ($)</Label>\r\n            <Input id="override-price" value={newPrice} onChange={(e) => setNewPrice(e.target.value)} placeholder="35.00" />\r\n          </div>\r\n          <Button onClick={() => void addOverride()} disabled={saving === "override"}>Set price</Button>\r\n        </Card>\r\n        {overrides.length === 0 ? (\r\n          <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">Nobody is on a special price. Everyone pays the event price.</p>\r\n        ) : (\r\n          <Card className="divide-y">\r\n            {overrides.map((row) => (\r\n              <div key={`${row.email}:${row.event_slug}`} className="flex items-center justify-between gap-4 p-3">\r\n                <div className="text-sm">\r\n                  <span className="font-medium">{row.email}</span>\r\n                  <span className="text-muted-foreground"> · {row.event_slug} · ${dollars(row.price_cents)}</span>\r\n                </div>\r\n                <Button variant="ghost" size="sm" onClick={() => void removeOverride(row)}>\r\n                  <Trash2 className="h-4 w-4" />\r\n                </Button>\r\n              </div>\r\n            ))}\r\n          </Card>\r\n        )}\r\n      </section>\r\n\r\n      <section className="space-y-4">\r\n        <h2 className="text-lg font-medium">What one student can see</h2>\r\n        <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">\r\n          A student with nothing set here sees every active offer. Turn one off and it disappears from their booking page.\r\n        </p>\r\n        <select className="h-10 rounded-md border border-input bg-background px-3 text-sm"\r\n          value={picked} onChange={(e) => setPicked(e.target.value)}>\r\n          <option value="">Choose a student</option>\r\n          {students.map((student) => (\r\n            <option key={student.class_id} value={student.class_id}>\r\n              {student.display_name || student.class_id}\r\n            </option>\r\n          ))}\r\n        </select>\r\n        {pickedStudent ? (\r\n          <Card className="divide-y">\r\n            {offers.filter((offer) => offer.active).map((offer) => {\r\n              const row = studentOffers.find((studentOffer) => studentOffer.offer_id === offer.id);\r\n              const on = row ? row.enabled : studentOffers.length === 0;\r\n              return (\r\n                <div key={offer.id} className="flex items-center justify-between gap-4 p-3">\r\n                  <div className="text-sm">\r\n                    <div className="font-medium">{offer.label}</div>\r\n                    <div className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{offer.id} · {offer.provider}</div>\r\n                  </div>\r\n                  <Switch checked={on} onCheckedChange={(next) => void setOfferForStudent(offer.id, next)} />\r\n                </div>\r\n              );\r\n            })}\r\n          </Card>\r\n        ) : null}\r\n      </section>\r\n    </main>\r\n  );\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction Field({ label, value, onChange, disabled }: {\r\n  label: string; value: string; onChange: (value: string) => void; disabled?: boolean;\r\n}) {\r\n  const id = `field-${label.replace(/\\W+/g, "-").toLowerCase()}`;\r\n  return (\r\n    <div className="w-28">\r\n      <Label htmlFor={id} className="text-xs">{label}</Label>\r\n      <Input id={id} value={value} disabled={disabled} onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.value)} />\r\n    </div>\r\n  );\r\n}\r\n\r\n'}]

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 3.2 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '-- One-off booking links: a single-use, single-price bookable session.\n--\n-- Joshua sends someone a URL, they book one session at a price set for them,\n-- and the link stops working. This needs no new booking machinery: an event type\n-- already carries length, notice, buffers, window and price, and every layer\n-- (page, edge function, slot policy, claim transaction) is already keyed by\n-- event slug. A one-off link IS a booking_event_types row with a random slug and\n-- single_use set.\n--\n-- Two things stood in the way and are fixed here.\n--\n-- 1. `single_session_bookings` still carried CHECK constraints from the day\n--    there was exactly one sellable thing: `end_at = start_at + 70 minutes` and\n--    `amount_cents = 4500`. Those made a custom-length or custom-price event\n--    impossible to insert. They also mean the already-seeded 30-minute free\n--    `consultation` event cannot currently be booked at all -- its insert\n--    violates the 70-minute check. Replacing them with a trigger that reads the\n--    event\'s own length fixes both.\n--\n-- 2. Nothing enforced single use. The same trigger does it, under a per-slug\n--    advisory lock so two people clicking the same link at the same moment\n--    cannot both get through.\n\n-- --------------------------------------------------------------------------\n-- The link itself\n-- --------------------------------------------------------------------------\nALTER TABLE public.booking_event_types\n  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS single_use boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false,\n  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS pinned_start_at timestamptz,\n  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS note text;\n\nCOMMENT ON COLUMN public.booking_event_types.single_use IS\n  \'A one-off link: once a booking against this slug is held or paid, the slug is spent and no further booking may be claimed.\';\nCOMMENT ON COLUMN public.booking_event_types.pinned_start_at IS\n  \'One exact start time this link sells. When set, the link offers only this time and the notice, window and weekly-hours rules do not apply -- Joshua already decided this time is fine. Double-booking is still refused by the claim transaction.\';\nCOMMENT ON COLUMN public.booking_event_types.note IS\n  \'Admin-only reminder of who a one-off link was made for. Never sent to the browser.\';\n\n-- A pinned time is a one-off idea: it names a single instant, so it can only be\n-- sold once.\nALTER TABLE public.booking_event_types\n  DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS booking_event_types_pinned_is_single_use_chk;\nALTER TABLE public.booking_event_types\n  ADD CONSTRAINT booking_event_types_pinned_is_single_use_chk\n  CHECK (pinned_start_at IS NULL OR single_use);\n\n-- --------------------------------------------------------------------------\n-- Duration and price stop being constants\n-- --------------------------------------------------------------------------\nALTER TABLE public.single_session_bookings\n  DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS single_session_duration_chk,\n  DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS single_session_amount_chk;\n\nALTER TABLE public.single_session_bookings\n  DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS single_session_amount_nonnegative_chk;\nALTER TABLE public.single_session_bookings\n  ADD CONSTRAINT single_session_amount_nonnegative_chk\n  CHECK (amount_cents IS NULL OR amount_cents >= 0);\n\n-- --------------------------------------------------------------------------\n-- What the dropped CHECKs used to say, said against the event instead\n-- --------------------------------------------------------------------------\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.enforce_booking_event_shape()\nRETURNS trigger\nLANGUAGE plpgsql\nSECURITY DEFINER\nSET search_path = public\nAS $$\nDECLARE\n  _event record;\n  _spent integer;\nBEGIN\n  SELECT * INTO _event\n  FROM public.booking_event_types\n  WHERE slug = NEW.event_slug;\n\n  IF NOT FOUND THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION \'unknown_event\' USING ERRCODE = \'P0400\';\n  END IF;\n\n  -- The length is the event\'s, not the caller\'s. This is the old 70-minute\n  -- CHECK, generalised: a consultation is 30 minutes and a one-off link is\n  -- whatever it was minted as.\n  IF NEW.end_at <> NEW.start_at + make_interval(mins => _event.length_minutes) THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION \'duration_mismatch\' USING ERRCODE = \'P0400\';\n  END IF;\n\n  IF _event.single_use THEN\n    -- Serialise every claim against this one link, so a simultaneous pair of\n    -- clicks cannot both read "unspent" and both insert. Taken before the read,\n    -- released at transaction end.\n    PERFORM pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtext(\'one-off-booking-link:\' || NEW.event_slug));\n\n    -- Spent means a booking exists that is live or was paid. A hold holds the\n    -- link (so nobody else can start one while a checkout is open); expiring or\n    -- abandoning that hold releases it again. A cancelled or refunded booking\n    -- stays spent -- the session was sold once, and a replacement gets a\n    -- replacement link.\n    SELECT count(*) INTO _spent\n    FROM public.single_session_bookings b\n    WHERE b.event_slug = NEW.event_slug\n      AND b.status NOT IN (\'expired\', \'abandoned\')\n      -- A retry of the same request is the same booking, not a second one.\n      AND b.client_idempotency_key <> NEW.client_idempotency_key;\n\n    IF _spent > 0 THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION \'link_already_used\' USING ERRCODE = \'P0410\';\n    END IF;\n  END IF;\n\n  RETURN NEW;\nEND;\n$$;\n\nDROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS enforce_booking_event_shape ON public.single_session_bookings;\nCREATE TRIGGER enforce_booking_event_shape\n  BEFORE INSERT ON public.single_session_bookings\n  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION public.enforce_booking_event_shape();\n\nREVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.enforce_booking_event_shape() FROM public, anon, authenticated;\n\n-- --------------------------------------------------------------------------\n-- Is a one-off link still open? Read by the booking page before it offers times.\n-- --------------------------------------------------------------------------\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.booking_event_is_spent(_event_slug text)\nRETURNS boolean\nLANGUAGE sql\nSTABLE\nSECURITY DEFINER\nSET search_path = public\nAS $$\n  SELECT EXISTS (\n    SELECT 1\n    FROM public.booking_event_types t\n    JOIN public.single_session_bookings b ON b.event_slug = t.slug\n    WHERE t.slug = _event_slug\n      AND t.single_use\n      AND b.status NOT IN (\'expired\', \'abandoned\')\n  );\n$$;\n\nREVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.booking_event_is_spent(text) FROM public, anon, authenticated;\nGRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.booking_event_is_spent(text) TO service_role;\n\n-- --------------------------------------------------------------------------\n-- A pinned link sells one instant, and only that instant\n--\n-- Everything this function normally asks -- is it far enough out, is it inside\n-- the rolling window, does it fall inside the weekly hours and on the slot grid\n-- -- exists to decide whether a time is one Joshua would agree to. A pinned\n-- link is that agreement, made in advance and by hand: he picked the time and\n-- sent it to one person. So the questions are answered, and the only thing left\n-- to check is that the caller is asking for the time on the link.\n--\n-- This does NOT relax the double-booking check. That lives in\n-- claim_single_session_slot, runs inside the booking transaction, and still\n-- refuses a slot that overlaps another booking or a calendar event.\n--\n-- Links without a pinned time keep the ordinary policy, reading their own\n-- minimum_notice_minutes and window_business_days -- which is how a short-notice\n-- link is made: mint it with a notice of zero.\n-- --------------------------------------------------------------------------\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.is_single_session_slot_bookable(\n  _start_at timestamptz,\n  _now timestamptz DEFAULT now(),\n  _event_slug text DEFAULT \'single-session\'\n) RETURNS boolean\nLANGUAGE plpgsql STABLE SET search_path = public AS $$\nDECLARE\n  _tz text := COALESCE(\n    (SELECT timezone FROM public.booking_availability_config WHERE id = 1),\n    \'America/Toronto\'\n  );\n  _event public.booking_event_types%ROWTYPE;\n  _length integer;\n  _pitch integer;\n  _local_start timestamp;\n  _local_date date;\n  _local_time time;\nBEGIN\n  SELECT * INTO _event FROM public.booking_event_types WHERE slug = _event_slug AND active;\n  IF NOT FOUND THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n\n  IF _start_at IS NULL THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n\n  -- The pinned instant, and nothing else. Still refuse to sell one in the past.\n  IF _event.pinned_start_at IS NOT NULL THEN\n    RETURN _start_at = _event.pinned_start_at AND _start_at > _now;\n  END IF;\n\n  _length := _event.length_minutes;\n  -- No explicit interval means slots start every length_minutes, which is what\n  -- Cal.com does and what produces the session\'s 1:00 / 2:10 / 3:20 chain.\n  _pitch := COALESCE(_event.slot_interval_minutes, _event.length_minutes);\n\n  IF _start_at < _now + make_interval(mins => _event.minimum_notice_minutes) THEN\n    RETURN false;\n  END IF;\n\n  _local_start := _start_at AT TIME ZONE _tz;\n  _local_date := _local_start::date;\n  _local_time := _local_start::time;\n\n  IF _local_date < (_now AT TIME ZONE _tz)::date\n     OR _local_date > public.single_session_booking_window_end(_now, _event_slug) THEN\n    RETURN false;\n  END IF;\n\n  RETURN EXISTS (\n    SELECT 1\n    FROM public.booking_availability a\n    WHERE a.day_of_week = extract(dow FROM _local_date)::integer\n      AND _local_time >= a.start_time\n      AND _local_time + make_interval(mins => _length) <= a.end_time\n      AND mod(\n        floor(extract(epoch FROM (_local_time - a.start_time)) / 60)::integer,\n        _pitch\n      ) = 0\n  );\nEXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN\n  -- Invalid timezone/configuration is not permission to sell a slot.\n  RETURN false;\nEND;\n$$;\n\nREVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.is_single_session_slot_bookable(timestamptz, timestamptz, text) FROM public, anon, authenticated;\nGRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.is_single_session_slot_bookable(timestamptz, timestamptz, text) TO service_role;\n\r\n'}]

Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.\n+\n+**Goal:** Ship one local, cross-platform `lsat-schedule` CLI and local MCP that give Joshua complete LSAT Prep scheduling control without changing Cal.com.\n+\n+**Architecture:** A new authenticated `scheduling-admin` Edge Function owns every privileged live mutation and returns typed snapshots. A small TypeScript client, domain types, preview builder, and command schemas are shared by the interactive CLI, direct CLI commands, and the local stdio MCP. PostgreSQL remains the scheduling source of truth and receives append-only audit rows plus undoable configuration state.\n+\n+**Tech Stack:** TypeScript, Node.js 22, `vite-node`, `@modelcontextprotocol/sdk`, Supabase Edge Functions (Deno), PostgreSQL migrations, Vitest, Deno tests.\n+\n+## Global Constraints\n+\n+- LSAT Prep only. Do not call, read, migrate, or modify Cal.com.\n+- The public booking page stays separate; visitors see their local timezone and operator commands use `America/Toronto`.\n+- Use the existing Google Calendar connection and existing Stripe hold, expiry, and refund paths.\n+- Existing booked sessions retain their original terms. Configuration changes affect future bookings only.\n+- Every CLI mutation shows exact before/after values and asks `Continue? y/N`; default is no change.\n+- MCP writes require preview, then a short-lived confirmation token tied to that exact preview.\n+- Deactivate links and passes; do not expose permanent deletion.\n+- Cancellation/reschedule messages default to no message. Create a draft only when requested; never send it automatically.\n+- Store no credentials in source, migrations, audit data, test fixtures, or command output.\n+\n+---\n+\n+## Locked file structure\n+\n+| Path | Responsibility |\n+|---|---|\n+| `supabase/migrations/<timestamp>_scheduling_control_center.sql` | Audit schema, date overrides, event schedule references, scoped RPCs, and undo transaction. |\n+| `supabase/functions/_shared/schedulingAdmin.ts` | Input validation, DTOs, preview token hashing, and action dispatch helpers that do not depend on HTTP. |\n+| `supabase/functions/scheduling-admin/index.ts` | Admin JWT gate and HTTP adapter for overview, previews, applies, and read-only scheduling actions. |\n+| `supabase/functions/scheduling-admin/index_test.ts` | Deno tests for admin authorization and action boundaries. |\n+| `scripts/scheduling/types.ts` | Shared Node-side command/action DTOs. |\n+| `scripts/scheduling/client.ts` | Authenticated calls to `scheduling-admin`; no terminal or MCP code. |\n+| `scripts/scheduling/preview.ts` | Human-readable before/after formatter and confirmation-token handling. |\n+| `scripts/scheduling/interactive.ts` | Readline menu and guided link, booking-pass, availability, booking, and undo flows. |\n+| `scripts/lsat-schedule.ts` | Direct-command parser and interactive CLI entry point. |\n+| `scripts/lsat-scheduling-mcp.ts` | Local stdio MCP server that maps tools to the shared client. |\n+| `bin/lsat-schedule.cjs` | Prefer your platform\'s native worktree tools. Fall back to manual git worktrees only when no native tool is available.\r\n\r\n**Core principle:** Detect existing isolation first. Then use native tools. Then fall back to git. Never fight the harness.\r\n\r\n**Announce at start:** "I\'m using the using-git-worktrees skill to set up an isolated workspace."\r\n\r\n## Step 0: Detect Existing Isolation\r\n\r\n**Before creating anything, check if you are already in an isolated workspace.**\r\n\r\n```bash\r\nGIT_DIR=$(cd "$(git rev-parse --git-dir)" 2>/dev/null && pwd -P)\r\nGIT_COMMON=$(cd "$(git rev-parse --git-common-dir)" 2>/dev/null && pwd -P)\r\nBRANCH=$(git branch --show-current)\r\n```\r\n\r\n**Submodule guard:** `GIT_DIR != GIT_COMMON` is also true inside git submodules. Before concluding "already in a worktree," verify you are not in a submodule:\r\n\r\n```bash\r\n# If this returns a path, you\'re in a submodule, not a worktree — treat as normal repo\r\ngit rev-parse --show-superproject-working-tree 2>/dev/null\r\n```\r\n\r\n**If `GIT_DIR != GIT_COMMON` (and not a submodule):** You are already in a linked worktree. Skip to Step 2 (Project Setup). Do NOT create another worktree.\r\n\r\nReport with branch state:\r\n- On a branch: "Already in isolated workspace at `<path>` on branch `<name>`."\r\n- Detached HEAD: "Already in isolated workspace at `<path>` (detached HEAD, externally managed). Branch creation needed at finish time."\r\n\r\n**If `GIT_DIR == GIT_COMMON` (or in a submodule):** You are in a normal repo checkout.\r\n\r\nHas the user already indicated their worktree preference in your instructions? If not, ask for consent before creating a worktree:\r\n\r\n> "Would you like me to set up an isolated worktree? It protects your current branch from changes."\r\n\r\nHonor any existing declared preference without asking. If the user declines consent, work in place and skip to Step 2.\r\n\r\n## Step 1: Create Isolated Workspace\r\n\r\n**You have two mechanisms. Try them in this order.**\r\n\r\n### 1a. Native Worktree Tools (preferred)\r\n\r\nThe user has asked for an isolated workspace (Step 0 consent). Do you already have a way to create a worktree? It might be a tool with a name like `EnterWorktree`, `WorktreeCreate`, a `/worktree` command, or a `--worktree` flag. If you do, use it and skip to Step 2.\r\n\r\nNative tools handle directory placement, branch creation, and cleanup automatically. Using `git worktree add` when you have a native tool creates phantom state your harness can\'t see or manage.\r\n\r\nOnly proceed to Step 1b if you have no native worktree tool available.\r\n\r\n### 1b. Git Worktree Fallback\r\n\r\n**Only use this if Step 1a does not apply** — you have no native worktree tool available. Create a worktree manually using git.\r\n\r\n#### Directory Selection\r\n\r\nFollow this priority order. Explicit user preference always beats observed filesystem state.\r\n\r\n1. **Check your instructions for a declared worktree directory preference.** If the user has already specified one, use it without asking.\r\n\r\n2. **Check for an existing project-local worktree directory:**\r\n   ```bash\r\n   ls -d .worktrees 2>/dev/null     # Preferred (hidden)\r\n   ls -d worktrees 2>/dev/null      # Alternative\r\n   ```\r\n   If found, use it. If both exist, `.worktrees` wins.\r\n\r\n3. **If there is no other guidance available**, default to `.worktrees/` at the project root.\r\n\r\n#### Safety Verification (project-local directories only)\r\n\r\n**MUST verify directory is ignored before creating worktree:**\r\n\r\n```bash\r\ngit check-ignore -q .worktrees 2>/dev/null || git check-ignore -q worktrees 2>/dev/null\r\n```\r\n\r\n**If NOT ignored:** Add to .gitignore, commit the change, then proceed.\r\n\r\n**Why critical:** Prevents accidentally committing worktree contents to repository.\r\n\r\n#### Create the Worktree\r\n\r\n```bash\r\n# Determine path based on chosen location\r\npath="$LOCATION/$BRANCH_NAME"\r\n\r\ngit worktree add "$path" -b "$BRANCH_NAME"\r\ncd "$path"\r\n```\r\n\r\n**Sandbox fallback:** If `git worktree add` fails with a permission error (sandbox denial), tell the user the sandbox blocked worktree creation and you\'re working in the current directory instead. Then run setup and baseline tests in place.\r\n\r\n## Step 2: Project Setup\r\n\r\nAuto-detect and run appropriate setup:\r\n\r\n```bash\r\n# Node.js\r\nif [ -f package.json ]; then npm install; fi\r\n\r\n# Rust\r\nif [ -f Cargo.toml ]; then cargo build; fi\r\n\r\n# Python\r\nif [ -f requirements.txt ]; then pip install -r requirements.txt; fi\r\nif [ -f pyproject.toml ]; then poetry install; fi\r\n\r\n# Go\r\nif [ -f go.mod ]; then go mod download; fi\r\n```\r\n\r\n## Step 3: Verify Clean Baseline\r\n\r\nRun tests to ensure workspace starts clean:\r\n\r\n```bash\r\n# Use project-appropriate command\r\nnpm test / cargo test / pytest / go test ./...\r\n```\r\n\r\n**If tests fail:** Report failures, ask whether to proceed or investigate.\r\n\r\n**If tests pass:** Report ready.\r\n\r\n### Report\r\n\r\n```\r\nWorktree ready at <full-path>\r\nTests passing (<N> tests, 0 failures)\r\nReady to implement <feature-name>\r\n```\r\n\r\n## Quick Reference\r\n\r\n| Situation | Action |\r\n|-----------|--------|\r\n| Already in linked worktree | Skip creation (Step 0) |\r\n| In a submodule | Treat as normal repo (Step 0 guard) |\r\n| Native worktree tool available | Use it (Step 1a) |\r\n| No native tool | Git worktree fallback (Step 1b) |\r\n| `.worktrees/` exists | Use it (verify ignored) |\r\n| `worktrees/` exists | Use it (verify ignored) |\r\n| Both exist | Use `.worktrees/` |\r\n| Neither exists | Check instruction file, then default `.worktrees/` |\r\n| Directory not ignored | Add to .gitignore + commit |\r\n| Permission error on create | Sandbox fallback, work in place |\r\n| Tests fail during baseline | Report failures + ask |\r\n| No package.json/Cargo.toml | Skip dependency install |\r\n\r\n## Common Mistakes\r\n\r\n### Fighting the harness\r\n\r\n- **Problem:** Using `git worktree add` when the platform already provides isolation\r\n- **Fix:** Step 0 detects existing isolation. Step 1a defers to native tools.\r\n\r\n### Skipping detection\r\n\r\n- **Problem:** Creating a nested worktree inside an existing one\r\n- **Fix:** Always run Step 0 before creating anything\r\n\r\n### Skipping ignore verification\r\n\r\n- **Problem:** Worktree contents get tracked, pollute git status\r\n- **Fix:** Always use `git check-ignore` before creating project-local worktree\r\n\r\n### Assuming directory location\r\n\r\n- **Problem:** Creates inconsistency, violates project conventions\r\n- **Fix:** Follow priority: explicit instructions > existing project-local directory > default\r\n\r\n### Proceeding with failing tests\r\n\r\n- **Problem:** Can\'t distinguish new bugs from pre-existing issues\r\n- **Fix:** Report failures, get explicit permission to proceed\r\n\r\n## Red Flags\r\n\r\n**Never:**\r\n- Create a worktree when Step 0 detects existing isolation\r\n- Use `git worktree add` when you have a native worktree tool (e.g., `EnterWorktree`). This is the #1 mistake — if you have it, use it.\r\n- Skip Step 1a by jumping straight to Step 1b\'s git commands\r\n- Create worktree without verifying it\'s ignored (project-local)\r\n- Skip baseline test verification\r\n- Proceed with failing tests without asking\r\n\r\n**Always:**\r\n- Run Step 0 detection first\r\n- Prefer native tools over git fallback\r\n- Follow directory priority: explicit instructions > existing project-local directory > default\r\n- Verify directory is ignored for project-local\r\n- Auto-detect and run project setup\r\n- Verify clean test baseline\r\n\r\n'}]

# Session Handoff — LSAT Prep scheduling CLI and local MCP

## Where it started

Joshua asked for a CLI or MCP that gives him full control of LSAT Prep scheduling, with no Cal.com integration. The scope became an installable command for Windows and Mac plus a local MCP, sharing one scheduling core.

The design and implementation plan are approved. Joshua authorized an isolated worktree, then requested this handoff before it was created.

## Decisions locked + what shipped

- LSAT Prep only — Cal.com is explicitly out of scope.
- Build one shared scheduling core with two local interfaces: `lsat-schedule` and a local stdio MCP.
- The CLI opens with a clear overview and guided menu; direct commands and `--json` remain available.
- CLI changes show exact before/after values and require `Continue? y/N`; MCP changes require preview then a confirmation token.
- Manage links, event settings, recurring availability, date overrides, bookings, refunds, booking passes, audit history, and undo.
- Standard paid link has a stable editable URL; custom links can be reusable, one-time, pinned, paid, or free.
- Booking passes are student-specific; standard single-session links are not.
- Existing bookings retain their booked terms. Refunds are separate from cancellations. Notification drafts are opt-in and never sent automatically.
- Keep the current Google Calendar configuration. Operators use `America/Toronto`; public booking pages render the visitor’s local timezone.
